Constants\nconstexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: fill\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Initialize array ****************************************************************************\nvoid fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrix\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CenSraKKbXJQhqwasrUJb","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01NfrNiLVGeaVrrg3kkex5Wx","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/llmtest/evals/20260901-162328/spmv_claude-opus-5-cc-medium_hybrid_r3/spmv/spmv.cpp","content":"// Hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA implementation of the SpMV benchmark.\n//\n// Parallelization strategy:\n//   * MPI   distributes the rows of the CSR matrix across ranks. The partition is\n//           balanced by number of non-zeros (not by number of rows) so that every\n//           rank receives roughly the same amount of work. Each rank only stores\n//           its own slice of the matrix.\n//   * CUDA  performs the actual SpMV. Every rank drives one GPU (selected by the\n//           rank's index within its compute node) and runs a CSR \"vector\" kernel\n//           with a sub-warp group per row; the group size is chosen at runtime\n//           from the average number of non-zeros per row.\n//   * OpenMP parallelizes all remaining host-side work (matrix rebasing, the\n//           reference solution and result verification) over the CPU cores.\n\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nusing index_t = uint32_t;\n\n// Constants\nconstexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;\n\n// CUDA launch configuration\nconstexpr int BLOCK_SIZE = 256;\nconstexpr int MAX_BLOCKS = 65535;\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                     \\\n    do {                                                                                     \\\n        const cudaError_t err__ = (call);                                                    \\\n        if (err__ != cudaSuccess) {                                                          \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err__), __FILE__, \\\n                    __LINE__);                                                               \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                    \\\n        }                                                                                    \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: fill\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Initialize array with random values\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   A: pointer to the array to initialize\n//   n: number of elements in the array\n//   maxVal: specifies range of random values [0, maxVal]\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrix\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//   (OpenMP parallel over the rows; each row is summed in the original order)\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n            const auto col = cols[j];\n            t += val[j] * vec[col];\n        }\n        out[i] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Kernel: spmvKernel\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   CSR SpMV on the GPU. A group of GROUP threads (a power-of-two subset of a\n//   warp) cooperates on one row: the group strides over the row's non-zeros for\n//   fully coalesced accesses and reduces the partial products with warp shuffles.\n//   The trip count of the outer loop is uniform across the grid so that every\n//   thread of a warp participates in the shuffles.\n// ****************************************************************************\ntemplate <int GROUP>\n__global__ void spmvKernel(const double* __restrict__ val, const index_t* __restrict__ cols,\n                           const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,\n                           const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t numRows,\n                           double* __restrict__ out) {\n    const index_t tid = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const int lane = static_cast<int>(tid & (GROUP - 1));\n    const index_t numGroups = (gridDim.x * blockDim.x) / GROUP;\n    const index_t firstRow = tid / GROUP;\n    const index_t numIter = (numRows + numGroups - 1) / numGroups;\n\n    for (index_t it = 0; it < numIter; ++it) {\n        const index_t row = firstRow + it * numGroups;\n        double sum = 0.0;\n        if (row < numRows) {\n            const index_t end = rowDelimiters[row + 1];\n            for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[row] + lane; j < end; j += GROUP) {\n                sum += val[j] * __ldg(&vec[cols[j]]);\n            }\n        }\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int off = GROUP / 2; off > 0; off >>= 1) {\n            sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, off, GROUP);\n        }\n        if (lane == 0 && row < numRows) {\n            out[row] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Launches the kernel variant whose per-row thread group best matches the\n// average number of non-zeros per row of this rank's slice.\nstatic void launchSpmv(const double* d_val, const index_t* d_cols, const index_t* d_rowDelimiters,\n                       const double* d_vec, const index_t numRows, double* d_out,\n                       const int group, cudaStream_t stream) {\n    if (numRows == 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n    const long long needed =\n        (static_cast<long long>(numRows) * group + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE;\n    const int blocks = static_cast<int>(needed > MAX_BLOCKS ? MAX_BLOCKS : needed);\n\n    switch (group) {\n        case 2:\n            spmvKernel<2><<<blocks, BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec,\n                                                             numRows, d_out);\n            break;\n        case 4:\n            spmvKernel<4><<<blocks, BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec,\n                                                             numRows, d_out);\n            break;\n        case 8:\n            spmvKernel<8><<<blocks, BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec,\n                                                             numRows, d_out);\n            break;\n        case 16:\n            spmvKernel<16><<<blocks, BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec,\n                                                              numRows, d_out);\n            break;\n        default:\n            spmvKernel<32><<<blocks, BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec,\n                                                              numRows, d_out);\n            break;\n    }\n}\n\nstatic int chooseGroupSize(const double avgNnzPerRow) {\n    if (avgNnzPerRow <= 3.0) return 2;\n    if (avgNnzPerRow <= 6.0) return 4;\n    if (avgNnzPerRow <= 12.0) return 8;\n    if (avgNnzPerRow <= 24.0) return 16;\n    return 32;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {\n    // Find the first mismatching index (if any) in parallel; report it like the\n    // serial version would.\n    index_t bad = size;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min : bad)\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n        const double ref = reference[i];\n        const double res = result[i];\n        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                bad = std::min(bad, i);\n            }\n        } else {\n            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                bad = std::min(bad, i);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (bad == size) {\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    const double ref = reference[bad];\n    const double res = result[bad];\n    if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", bad, ref, res);\n    } else {\n        printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\\n\",\n               bad, ref, res, std::abs((res - ref) / ref));\n    }\n    return false;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);\n\n    int rank = 0;\n    int numRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);\n\n    index_t numRows = 1024;\n    index_t sparsity = 10;\n    index_t iterations = 10;\n    double maxVal = 1.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numRows = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-m\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            maxVal = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Calculate number of non-zero elements\n    const index_t nItems = (numRows * numRows) / sparsity;\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Matrix size: %u x %u\\n\", numRows, numRows);\n        printf(\"Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\\n\", sparsity);\n        printf(\"Non-zero elements: %u (%.2f%% sparse)\\n\",\n               nItems, 100.0 * (1.0 - static_cast<double>(nItems) / (numRows * numRows)));\n        printf(\"Iterations: %u\\n\", iterations);\n        printf(\"Max value: %.2f\\n\", maxVal);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n        printf(\"MPI ranks: %d, OpenMP threads/rank: %d\\n\", numRanks, ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    MPI_Comm nodeComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;\n    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm);\n    int localRank = 0;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &localRank);\n    MPI_Comm_free(&nodeComm);\n\n    int deviceCount = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));\n    if (deviceCount == 0) {\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            fprintf(stderr, \"No CUDA devices available\\n\");\n        }\n        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(nullptr));  // establish the context up front\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Data generation (kept sequential on rank 0 to reproduce the exact\n    // random sequence of the original benchmark) and distribution.\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    std::vector<double> h_val;            // Non-zero values (rank 0: full matrix)\n    std::vector<index_t> h_cols;          // Column indices  (rank 0: full matrix)\n    std::vector<index_t> h_rowDelimiters; // Row delimiters  (rank 0: full matrix)\n    std::vector<double> h_vec(numRows);   // Dense vector (replicated on all ranks)\n    std::vector<double> h_out;            // Output vector (rank 0)\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        h_val.resize(nItems);\n        h_cols.resize(nItems);\n        h_rowDelimiters.resize(numRows + 1);\n        h_out.resize(numRows);\n\n        printf(\"Initializing data structures...\\n\");\n        fill(h_vec.data(), numRows, maxVal);\n        fill(h_val.data(), nItems, maxVal);\n        initRandomMatrix(h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), nItems, numRows);\n    }\n\n    MPI_Bcast(h_vec.data(), static_cast<int>(numRows), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    // Row partition, balanced by number of non-zeros.\n    std::vector<index_t> rowStart(numRanks + 1);\n    std::vector<index_t> nnzStart(numRanks + 1);\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        rowStart[0] = 0;\n        for (int r = 1; r < numRanks; ++r) {\n            const index_t target =\n                static_cast<index_t>((static_cast<uint64_t>(nItems) * r) / numRanks);\n            // First row whose starting offset reaches the target nnz count\n            index_t lo = rowStart[r - 1];\n            index_t hi = numRows;\n            while (lo < hi) {\n                const index_t mid = lo + (hi - lo) / 2;\n                if (h_rowDelimiters[mid] < target) {\n                    lo = mid + 1;\n                } else {\n                    hi = mid;\n                }\n            }\n            rowStart[r] = lo;\n        }\n        rowStart[numRanks] = numRows;\n        for (int r = 0; r <= numRanks; ++r) {\n            nnzStart[r] = h_rowDelimiters[rowStart[r]];\n        }\n    }\n    MPI_Bcast(rowStart.data(), numRanks + 1, MPI_UINT32_T, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    MPI_Bcast(nnzStart.data(), numRanks + 1, MPI_UINT32_T, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    const index_t myFirstRow = rowStart[rank];\n    const index_t myRows = rowStart[rank + 1] - myFirstRow;\n    const index_t myNnz = nnzStart[rank + 1] - nnzStart[rank];\n\n    std::vector<int> rowCounts(numRanks), rowDispls(numRanks);\n    std::vector<int> nnzCounts(numRanks), nnzDispls(numRanks);\n    for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {\n        rowCounts[r] = static_cast<int>(rowStart[r + 1] - parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < myRows; ++i) {\n        l_rowDelimiters[i] -= base;\n    }\n    l_rowDelimiters[myRows] = myNnz;\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Device buffers\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    double* d_val = nullptr;\n    index_t* d_cols = nullptr;\n    index_t* d_rowDelimiters = nullptr;\n    double* d_vec = nullptr;\n    double* d_out = nullptr;\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_val, (myNnz ? myNnz : 1) * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_cols, (myNnz ? myNnz : 1) * sizeof(index_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_rowDelimiters, (myRows + 1) * sizeof(index_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_vec, (numRows ? numRows : 1) * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_out, (myRows ? myRows : 1) * sizeof(double)));\n\n    if (myNnz > 0) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_val, l_val.data(), myNnz * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n        CUDA_CHECK(\n            cudaMemcpy(d_cols, l_cols.data(), myNnz * sizeof(index_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_rowDelimiters, l_rowDelimiters.data(), (myRows + 1) * sizeof(index_t),\n                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_vec, h_vec.data(), numRows * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    const int group =\n        chooseGroupSize(myRows > 0 ? static_cast<double>(myNnz) / static_cast<double>(myRows) : 1.0);\n\n    // For validation, compute reference solution\n    std::vector<double> h_reference;\n    if (validate && rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Computing reference solution...\\n\");\n        h_reference.resize(numRows);\n        spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(),\n                h_vec.data(), numRows, h_reference.data());\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Perform SpMV computation\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Computing SpMV...\\n\");\n    }\n\n    // Warm up the kernel / JIT path outside of the measured region\n    launchSpmv(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec, myRows, d_out, group, 0);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());\n\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    for (index_t iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) The partition is\n//           balanced by number of non-zeros (not by number of rows) so that every\n//           rank receives roughly the same amount of work. Each rank only stores\n//           its own slice of the matrix.\n//   * CUDA  performs the actual SpMV. Every rank drives one GPU (selected by the\n//           rank's index within its compute node) and runs a CSR \"vector\" kernel\n//           with a sub-warp group per row; the group size is chosen at runtime\n//           from the average number of non-zeros per row.\n//   * OpenMP parallelizes all remaining host-side work (matrix rebasing, the\n//           reference solution and result verification) over the CPU cores.\n\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nusing index_t = uint32_t;\n\n// Constants\nconstexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;\n\n// CUDA launch configuration\nconstexpr int BLOCK_SIZE = 256;\nconstexpr int MAX_BLOCKS = 65535;\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                     \\\n    do {                                                                                     \\\n        const cudaError_t err__ = (call);                                                    \\\n        if (err__ != cudaSuccess) {                                                          \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err__), __FILE__, \\\n                    __LINE__);                                                               \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                    \\\n        }                                                                                    \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: fill\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Initialize array with random values\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   A: pointer to the array to initialize\n//   n: number of elements in the array\n//   maxVal: specifies range of random values [0, maxVal]\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrix\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//   (OpenMP parallel over the rows; each row is summed in the original order)\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n            const auto col = cols[j];\n            t += val[j] * vec[col];\n        }\n        out[i] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Kernel: spmvKernel\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   CSR SpMV on the GPU. A group of GROUP threads (a power-of-two subset of a\n//   warp) cooperates on one row: the group strides over the row's non-zeros for\n//   fully coalesced accesses and reduces the partial products with warp shuffles.\n//   The trip count of the outer loop is uniform across the grid so that every\n//   thread of a warp participates in the shuffles.\n// ****************************************************************************\ntemplate <int GROUP>\n__global__ void spmvKernel(const double* __restrict__ val, const index_t* __restrict__ cols,\n                           const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,\n                           const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t numRows,\n                           double* __restrict__ out) {\n    const index_t tid = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const int lane = static_cast<int>(tid & (GROUP - 1));\n    const index_t numGroups = (gridDim.x * blockDim.x) / GROUP;\n    const index_t firstRow = tid / GROUP;\n    const index_t numIter = (numRows + numGroups - 1) / numGroups;\n\n    for (index_t it = 0; it < numIter; ++it) {\n        const index_t row = firstRow + it * numGroups;\n        double sum = 0.0;\n        if (row < numRows) {\n            const index_t end = rowDelimiters[row + 1];\n            for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[row] + lane; j < end; j += GROUP) {\n                sum += val[j] * __ldg(&vec[cols[j]]);\n            }\n        }\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int off = GROUP / 2; off > 0; off >>= 1) {\n            sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, off, GROUP);\n        }\n        if (lane == 0 && row < numRows) {\n            out[row] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Launches the kernel variant whose per-row thread group best matches the\n// average number of non-zeros per row of this rank's slice.\nstatic void launchSpmv(const double* d_val, const index_t* d_cols, const index_t* d_rowDelimiters,\n                       const double* d_vec, const index_t numRows, double* d_out,\n                       const int group, cudaStream_t stream) {\n    if (numRows == 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n    const long long needed =\n        (static_cast<long long>(numRows) * group + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE;\n    const int blocks = static_cast<int>(needed > MAX_BLOCKS ? MAX_BLOCKS : needed);\n\n    switch (group) {\n        case 2:\n            spmvKernel<2><<<blocks, BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec,\n                                                             numRows, d_out);\n            break;\n        case 4:\n            spmvKernel<4><<<blocks, BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec,\n                                                             numRows, d_out);\n            break;\n        case 8:\n            spmvKernel<8><<<blocks, BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec,\n                                                             numRows, d_out);\n            break;\n        case 16:\n            spmvKernel<16><<<blocks, BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec,\n                                                              numRows, d_out);\n            break;\n        default:\n            spmvKernel<32><<<blocks, BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec,\n                                                              numRows, d_out);\n            break;\n    }\n}\n\nstatic int chooseGroupSize(const double avgNnzPerRow) {\n    if (avgNnzPerRow <= 3.0) return 2;\n    if (avgNnzPerRow <= 6.0) return 4;\n    if (avgNnzPerRow <= 12.0) return 8;\n    if (avgNnzPerRow <= 24.0) return 16;\n    return 32;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {\n    // Find the first mismatching index (if any) in parallel; report it like the\n    // serial version would.\n    index_t bad = size;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min : bad)\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n        const double ref = reference[i];\n        const double res = result[i];\n        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                bad = std::min(bad, i);\n            }\n        } else {\n            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                bad = std::min(bad, i);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (bad == size) {\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    const double ref = reference[bad];\n    const double res = result[bad];\n    if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", bad, ref, res);\n    } else {\n        printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\\n\",\n               bad, ref, res, std::abs((res - ref) / ref));\n    }\n    return false;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);\n\n    int rank = 0;\n    int numRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);\n\n    index_t numRows = 1024;\n    index_t sparsity = 10;\n    index_t iterations = 10;\n    double maxVal =  } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Calculate number of non-zero elements\n    const index_t nItems = (numRows * numRows) / sparsity;\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Matrix size: %u x %u\\n\", numRows, numRows);\n        printf(\"Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\\n\", sparsity);\n CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(nullptr));  // establish the context up front\n\n    // h_rowDelimiters.resize(numRows + 1);\n        h_out.resize(numRows);\n\n        printf(\"Initializing data structures...\\n\");\n        fill(h_vec.data(), numRows, maxVal);\n    // First row whose starting offset reaches the target nnz count\n            index_t lo = rowStart[r - 1];\n            index_t hi = numRows;\n            while (lo < hi) {\n                const index_t mid = lo + (hi - lo) / 2;\n                if (h_rowDelimiters[mid] < target) {\n                    lo = mid + 1;\n                } else {\n                    hi = mid;\n                }\n index_t* d_cols = nullptr;\n    index_t* d_rowDelimiters = nullptr;\n    double* d_vec = nullptr;\n    double* d_out = nullptr;\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_val, (myNnz ? myNnz : 1) * CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_vec, (numRows ? numRows : 1) * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_out, (myRows ? myRows : 1) * sizeof(double)));\n\n    if (myNnz > 0) {\n CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_vec, h_vec.data(), numRows * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    const int group =\n        chooseGroupSize(myRows > 0 ? static_cast<double>(myNnz) / static_cast<double>(myRows) : 1.0);\n\n    // For validation, compute reference solution\n    std::vector<double> h_reference;\n    if (validate && rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Computing reference array to initialize\n//   n: number of elements in the array\n//   maxVal: specifies range of random values [0, maxVal]\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrix\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n            const auto col = cols[j];\n            t += val[j] * vec[col];\n        }\n        out[i] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n        const double ref = reference[i];\n        const double res = result[i];\n        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n            // For very small values, check absolute error\n            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", i, ref, res);\n                return false;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // Check relative error\n            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, ref, res, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    index_t numRows = 1024;\n    index_t sparsity = 10;\n    index_t iterations = 10;\n    double maxVal = 1.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numRows = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-m\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            maxVal = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Calculate number of non-zero elements\n    const index_t nItems = (numRows * numRows) / sparsity;\n\n    printf(\"Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Matrix size: %u x %u\\n\", numRows, numRows);\n    printf(\"Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\\n\", sparsity);\n    printf(\"Non-zero elements: %u (%.2f%% sparse)\\n\", \n           nItems, 100.0 * (1.0 - static_cast<double>(nItems) / (numRows * numRows)));\n    printf(\"Iterations: %u\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Max value: %.2f\\n\", maxVal);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    // Allocate and initialize data structures\n    std::vector<double> h_val(nItems);                  // Non-zero values\n    std::vector<index_t> h_cols(nItems);                // Column indices\n    std::vector<index_t> h_rowDelimiters(numRows + 1);  // Row delimiters\n    std::vector<double> h_vec(numRows);                 // Dense vector\n    std::vector<double> h_out(numRows);                 // Output vector\n\n    printf(\"Initializing data structures...\\n\");\n    fill(h_vec.data(), numRows, maxVal);\n    fill(h_val.data(), nItems, maxVal);\n    initRandomMatrix(h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), nItems, numRows);\n\n    // For validation, compute reference solution\n    std::vector<double> h_reference;\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Computing reference solution...\\n\");\n        h_reference.resize(numRows);\n        spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), \n                h_vec.data(), numRows, h_reference.data());\n    }\n\n    // Perform SpMV computation\n    printf(\"Computing SpMV...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    for (index_t iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n        spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(),\n                h_vec.data(), numRows, h_out.data());\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n    \n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    const double gflops = (2.0 * nItems * iterations) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n    const double avgTime = duration.count() / static_cast<double>(iterations);\n    \n    printf(\"Average time per iteration: %.3f ms\\n\", avgTime);\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(h_out, \"OutputVector\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        const bool valid = verifyResults(h_reference.data(), h_out.data(), numRows);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n}\n","userModified":false}}

Ranks pick their GPU by their index within `MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED`, so one rank per GPU maps automatically.\n- **CUDA** — the timed kernel is a CSR \"vector\" kernel: a power-of-two thread group per row strides over the row's non-zeros (coalesced) and reduces with `__shfl_down_sync`; `vec` goes through `__ldg`. Group size (2/4/8/16/32) is picked at runtime from the average nnz/row. The outer grid-stride loop has a grid-uniform trip count so every warp lane participates in the shuffles.\n- **OpenMP** — parallelizes all host-side work: the reference solution, verification (min-reduction to report the first failing index, as the serial version does), and the CSR rebasing.\n\n**Measured** (4× RTX 3090, `-n 32768 -i 200`):\n\n| ranks/GPUs | time/iter | GFLOPS |\n|---|---|---|\n| 1 | 1.529 ms | 140 |\n| 2 | 0.787 ms | 273 |\n| 4 | 0.434 ms | 495 |\n\nNear-linear scaling; single-GPU throughput is ~840 GB/s of effective bandwidth, i.e. ~90% of the 3090's peak, so the kernel is at the memory-bound limit. Serial baseline for the same problem is 168 ms/iter (~390× on 4 GPUs).\n\n**Correctness** — `-v` passes across sizes (including edge cases: `nItems == 0` for `-n 3`, ranks with zero rows, very sparse matrices, oversubscribed GPUs). Comparing `-r` output against the original serial build, Sum/Min/Max/Samples agree to ~1e-15 relative; the FNV hash differs, as expected, because the GPU sums each row in a different order.\n\nTwo deliberate deviations worth flagging: matrix generation stays serial on rank 0 because `rand()` is a sequential stream and parallelizing it would change the generated matrix; and the GFLOPS/avg-time lines are now computed from a double-precision timer instead of the truncated integer milliseconds, which avoids printing `inf` when a fast run rounds to 0 ms (the `Computation time: %ld ms` line is unchanged)."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":404,"cache_read_input_tokens":59754,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":404},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ac2e5395-85b0-401f-8a9f-001da078cc61","uuid":"67f59673-2834-45ed-9c76-2efb5490097e","timestamp":"2026-09-06T15:52:46.590Z","request_id":"req_011CenTJ9VJcsVc8z3FPtmAw"}
